r2sleigh Roadmap
================

The one ordered list: what is left, who owns it, how its exit is checked, and
in what order. Design lives in the ADRs each row links; the why in
[doc/engine-vision.md](doc/engine-vision.md); the working rules in
[AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md). Where this file and an ADR disagree, this file wins and
the disagreement is a defect. Done work and dated measurements live in
[doc/roadmap-history.md](doc/roadmap-history.md) and the commits, not here.

`r2s` is the tool: one engine (discovery, lifting, SSA, memory, types, a
certifying decompiler) under radare2's command language, a visual mode, and an
agent surface. It prints C only where checked facts justify it; otherwise a
counted residual or a refusal that says why.

Rows cite the [end-to-end audit](doc/reviews/2026-10-end-to-end-audit.md) as
`audit W1` (correctness), `M` (missing), `D` (design), `P` (performance), `B`
(build) and `T` (tests): always with the word, since the roadmap uses the same
letters. Each audit row has exactly one owning item (table at the end). The
[2026-11 hand audit](doc/reviews/2026-11-hand-audit.md) is cited as
`hand audit` and its section number; its findings are rows D150 to D158.

Gates
-----

In order of authority. A benchmark score is never proof; output is read by
hand before a quality claim. The third column is what the gate cannot see
today; GT closes it.

| Gate | Question | Blind spot (audit) |
|------|----------|--------------------|
| Equivalence (`tests/equiv`) | Does the C compute what the machine code computes? The north star. | before a residual inside the body, only the output is compared, not memory (T1's rest: `pddj` states no residual's position but `unproven-return`'s); stripped builds only, so the DWARF route is never run (T5) |
| Census (`tests/db`, every coverage, pinned and stripped function) | Did any rendering move? A structural change is byte-identical or names each moved line. | — |
| Certification (`scripts/certify_render.py`) | Does every rendering read only what it assigns, and never panic? | grades 98 of 1,492 functions, none stripped; cannot see invented arguments (T6) |
| Source-gold (`scripts/differential_truth.py`) | Are signatures what the source declared, or marked? | soundness 24% and 9% on review.c and branchy.c at -O2 (D70; the 43% and 21% of W49 counted C runtime functions as truth and never graded a function whose text opens with a struct); `pddj` states no basis, so it cannot mark by grade (D70); `frame_agree.py` in no gate (T21) |
| Coverage (`tests/coverage`) | How much of a binary renders; does any regress? | stale baseline; clang other than 17 ungated (T19) |
| Differential (`scripts/diff_r2.py`) | Where discovery, naming, decoding disagree with radare2, and who is right. | in CI against radare2 6.2.4 on elf, mach0 and pe, every disagreement judged and radare2-only values ratcheted; `ir iz iee i f` not yet compared (T4) |
| Dylints (`tools/dylints`) | Does the code keep the structural rules? | 77 of 79 warn-only, their count ratcheted in CI; every path filter names a live path, tested (T3) |
| Structure (`scripts/structure-report.sh`) | Did nesting, length, arguments or clones rise? | — |

Where this stands (2026-10-14, master 98dd6e06)
-----------------------------------------------

| Measure | Value |
|---------|-------|
| Equivalence (blessed baselines) | x86-64 678 of 756 equal, aarch64 685; no `differs`, `ub`, `uninit` or compile error; the rest `residual-trap` (57, 50), `unsupported` (20) and `slow` (1) |
| Tests | 2,414 workspace tests, none flaky over 7 runs; Kani 20 of 20 |
| Rendering | `pdd` renders 94% of `ls` and 79% of static glibc; r2dec 18.1k lines |
| Robustness | about 6,000 hostile and fuzzed runs, one panic (broken pipe, audit W52); every rerun, call order, session against fresh open and Mac against Linux byte-identical |
| Wrong output (audit: 166 rows, 63 of them wrong output; 18 high: 15 wrong output, M1, P5, P6) | container facts (PE mapping and imports, ET_REL), AArch64 lift semantics, invented call arguments, functions absorbed across CFI-only starts |
| Cost | large x86 `pdd` 13 to 26 G instructions and 188 to 256 MB, callee preparation 47 to 84% of it; pumasim `afl` 20 G and 158 MB; worst cases gzdoom aarch64 51 s (audit P5) and r2pay `init1` 1.02 GB (audit P6) |
| Structure | `long_comments` 2609, `excessive_nesting` 460 |

The 2026-10-04 review rated the code 4/10 for weak algorithms and structure,
hence algorithms and structure before features. The 2026-10-14 audit adds
that the inputs are not yet right: container facts and lift semantics feed
every view, so they go first. The 2026-11-09 hand audit (about 460 functions
read against source, DWARF, radare2, Ghidra and kuna) found the C that renders
right and the losses in six owners: a switch's dispatch block, library rows,
C++ landing pads, the cost of `aa`, result width and presence, and frame
boundaries. Its first moves are step 7 of the order.

Decisions
---------

| | Decision | State |
|-|----------|-------|
| D1 | Restructure, not rewrite: one item per branch replaces its owner and deletes the old path in the same branch; the gates run once at the item's exit, where the census diff is read | standing |
| D2 | Stable identity before more facts | done (F1) |
| D3 | Stages are types | done (F1) |
| D4 | Provenance on every fact, as `Fact<T>` | C |
| D5 | Gates are blessed in CI, never on a laptop | standing |
| D6 | Equivalence runs on arm64 too | standing |
| D7 | The visual mode never calls the engine while drawing | done (V1) |
| D8 | The observation journal is replaced by D, not kept | done (D) |
| D9 | Location SSA is superseded | done |
| D10 | DecBench is measured again only where PyPI is reachable | standing |
| D11 | No new iteration off the fixpoint driver or a stated worklist; no silent cap; no entity-keyed map; no cache outside the query database. Dylints enforce each as its owner lands | standing |
| D12 | One IR, indexed once ([one-ir](doc/adr-one-ir.md)) | F2 |
| D13 | One query database ([query-database](doc/adr-query-database.md)) | Q |
| D14 | One frame model ([frame-model](doc/adr-frame-model.md)) | P4 |
| D15 | One machine profile from the trusted Sleigh bundle; no architecture-name match below the lifter ([machine-profile](doc/adr-machine-profile.md)) | M |
| D16 | One byte relation ([byte-relation](doc/adr-byte-relation.md)) | B |
| D17 | No new crates: a layer boundary inside a crate is a module boundary enforced by a Dylint; the IR depends on neither the lifter nor type inference | standing; L6 meets its second clause |
| D18 | Untrusted input is bounded: a read stops at the bytes the file holds, and every pass states its cost | standing (H) |
| D19 | Performance is a gate: release time, peak RSS and allocation count budgeted on named workloads in CI | standing (PF) |
| D20 | The decompiler is rewritten, not refactored ([decompiler-rewrite](doc/adr-decompiler-rewrite.md)) | done (D) |
| D21 | The source language is a profile; the IR, facts and proof name no language ([language-profile](doc/adr-language-profile.md)) | LP |
| D22 | Every pass is an index with a stated bound: `O((V + E) · h)` or says at its definition why not ([next-pass](doc/adr-next-pass.md)) | LX |
| D23 | Summaries compose over the call graph: a body is prepared once per session and held; no `pdd` pays a transitive closure | SM |
| D24 | The lift is a query per address over a compact IL | LF |
| D25 | Types are one monotone system: union-find, one meet per class, incremental | T |
| D26 | Allocation is budgeted: one arena per function, indexes sized at the seal, the allocator chosen by measurement | PF, MM |
| D27 | Contract first, then the layers behind it, on one integration branch | done (`rebuild` merged, #142) |
| D28 | A defect in a vendored Sleigh specification is fixed in `vendor/` with an `r2il::eval` regression and listed in `vendor/README.md`; it is reported upstream, or resynced where upstream already fixed it (the `addv` precedent, c886c13c) | VC |
| D29 | Every refusal is a record: an analysis that stops (convention, capture, walk, image) yields a typed reason the verbs print and `pddj` carries, exit status 0; never a bare `Err` string | RF |
| D30 | A gate grades what it claims or it is red: a check that compares nothing, a lint aimed at a deleted path, a silent skip and an unjudged disagreement are defects in the gate | GT |
| D31 | Every layer has an executable oracle: the container against radare2 and DWARF, the lift against the CPU, each IR rewrite against `r2il::eval` before and after it, the C against the machine. A layer without one is not admitted to render (2026-10-14) | standing (GT, VC) |
| D32 | `pdd` renders an architecture only where an equivalence target grades it; elsewhere the listing, discovery and references work and `pdd` refuses with that reason (2026-10-14: ARM32 and RISC-V 64 refuse until LS and M give them targets) | standing (RF refuses; M, LS add targets) |
| D33 | `libsla-sys`'s Ghidra, the compiler `sleigh-compiler` builds against its headers, and `sleigh-config`'s specifications sit on one Ghidra commit; the engine resyncs whenever the specifications do (2026-10-15: master `918acd744d`) | standing (VC; met 2026-10-16: all five processors on `918acd744d`) |
| D34 | A lift pass over one instruction's ops edits through one plan whose applier keeps every instruction-relative label on its op, and `decode_sweep` runs each pass's input and output through `r2il::eval` from the same states ([verified layers](doc/adr-verified-layers.md)) | done (LS, 2026-10-16): `plan::Plan` is the one applier of `whole_unique_reads`, `canonicalize_memory_operands`, `split_odd_register_zeroes`, `forward_repeated_loads` and user-operation expansion (the last three kept no label); `R2ILOp::local_offset` owns the label codec and `r2il::eval::run` follows it; `decode_sweep` runs every pass's input and output from four seeded states, user operations opaque except in the expansion pass, which is compared only where the run skips the operation (edited 0 on x86-64 and aarch64, 498 and 533 on ARM32; its semantics stay with `packed_extension`, `word_shuffle`, `neon_*` and the AArch64 oracle, the ADR rows it partial), and failed three mutants (a label not moved, a store dropped, a piece read a byte off), a per-language floor of the passes that must edit an instruction eval ran (a no-op `forward_repeated_loads` passed before it), `plan::tests` adding random programs with neutral ops inserted around forward branches; 1.2M random lifts in eight languages and the 429 goldens byte-identical, release `pdd` on 0pack and pumasim 0.3% and 0.6% fewer instructions, peak memory flat. `internal_control` is not on the plan (it resolves labels and departs from the specification at a zero BSF/BSR source) and keeps `local_control_tests`, now on `eval::run`; ARM's immediate `vmov`/`vorr`/`vbic` read a local through a `Load` from the const space, now a `Copy` in `translate_load` (a width change refuses), and the lift ends in `r2il::validate_block` so invalid IL is `LiftError::Invalid` (2026-10-16): `arm`, `arm-be`, `arm-thumb` and `arm-thumb-be` words join the holder gate and a direct big-endian `whole_unique_reads` test judges the insert from the high end, mutants failing them (a dropped write-back, the big-endian shift; an off-by-one above the piece survives the ARM words, whose holder starts at zero, and fails only the direct test), `decode_sweep` 7 s debug, the 430 goldens unmoved, release `pdd` instructions retired +0.0% on 0pack and +0.3% on pumasim (validating first cost +1.0% and +2.6% until `validate_op` built its issue paths only for an issue), peak RSS flat; conditional: gate it in the sweep once its departures are listed, until then the exclusive and NEON idioms are compared only up to their opaque user operation; next: a test-side reference per `ModelledUserOperation`, as `packed_extension::expected` is, so the sweep compares the expansion's output and joins the floor |
| D35 | x86-64 has a CPU oracle recorded on contabo and replayed on every host, and CI fails a change to a vendored `.sinc`, `.slaspec` or `.pspec` that adds no oracle case or `r2il::eval` test (D28 enforced) | done (VC, 2026-10-16: 374 cases; `scripts/spec_evidence.py`) |
| D36 | A temporary is named by where it is defined (its instruction and its place in that instruction's own layout), not by its unique-space offset, so a resync that changes no instruction's operations moves no census line | done (2026-10-16: r2il lays out, r2ssa names; gate `stable_temporaries`) |
| D37 | r2ssa states why an arity is a floor, one typed variant per floor branch (an unresolved transfer, a supervisor entry, an argument area unplaced or read past an unproven register prefix, a register slot read past the prefix, a call of unproven arity: indirect, an unresolved cycle member, a missing prototype), an engine test each; r2engine reads the cause, and a cycle's results are the same fixpoint as its arities | P7; the typed cause and the walk-free cycle done (2026-10-16: `SourceArityFloor`, nine engine tests, one per cause and call split); done, results half (2026-10-16): an unproven result states its causes (`SourceUnprovenResult`: eight `SourceResultCause` variants, `result_owners` and `Owners` deleted), a seed carrier from the paths of its own, and a cycle's results are the same worklist on every request's result demand; 14 engine tests (`results.rs`: one per cause, the pairs on x86-64 and AArch64, the three that stay unproven); S6 (2026-11-10): the causes stay one typed variant each, `Homed` added (a register past the parameters only homed, -O0), `ArgumentAreaPastBudget` added (a stack read past 64 unread slots; the slots below stand, `arity.rs` tests the hostile load); `RegisterRead` is no longer minted, since a read past a section's parameters now makes them parameters (D165), and D37's probe of the first slot past each section names only the calls that slot reaches |
| D38 | The cost verdict counts retired user-space instructions (`perf_event_open`; `--measure instructions`); each record and baseline states its measure and budget and the ratchet never compares across them; wall time is only a hang guard. An emulated target stays on `time` until a counting qemu exists: the packaged one has no `-plugin`, and `perf_event_open` is `ENOSYS` in its guest The stack-exhaustion exemption (a vector the original survives, faulted at the stack by both renderings, is not graded) can hide a rendering that recurses where the original returns; the closing step is to grade such a vector through a rendering built with sibling-call optimisation on, so a value difference or a real runaway still reads `differs`. The nearest wall bound is the x86 `Equivalence gcc-O2 a` shard's `timeout -s ABRT 8m`: 455 s at #327's head against 329 s at its base, and it aborted at about 489 s twice on older heads of that PR, so 25 s of margin stands between a green stack and a red one; until the instruction gate replaces the wall bound, split that shard (the clang shards are unchanged) before the next PR that adds equivalence work. | GT (counter and gate done 2026-10-16; the baseline flip and the emulated count next) |
| D39 | A scheduled CI job reports how far the Ghidra base, `TESTBINS_REF` and the judged radare2 release are behind upstream and fails past a stated budget | done (GT, 2026-10-16: `scripts/upstream_drift.py` and the weekly `upstream-drift` job; every processor's pin is read from `vendor/README.md`, a blobless clone counts the commits each pin lacks over the paths its copy vendors, and a vendored file equal to another upstream version refutes the pin; budgets 90 days Ghidra, 180 `TESTBINS_REF`, 90 radare2; first run red by design: ARM, MIPS, RISC-V and x86 lack commits 458, 422, 491 and 443 days old until VC's resync, and AArch64's `.ldefs`, `.opinion` and two `.cspec` files are older than its `918acd744d` pin, a resync of five files owed to VC next; the Ghidra base, `TESTBINS_REF` and radare2 6.2.4 are within budget) |
| D41 | A property of an `R2ILOp` has one owner in r2il, stated as an exhaustive list; a match elsewhere that derives one is a Dylint error (`r2il_op_property_outside_r2il`; #287 showed the class). The `SSAOp` call class has its owner in `SSAOp::transfer`/`is_call` (every other operation named by four class macros), about 35 copies read it, and `ssa_call_class_outside_op` denies a pattern naming `Call` or `CallInd` behind a default. It found `stack_protector`'s `fails_without_return`, which matched a direct `Call` only and dropped a `-fno-plt` `call *slot` (fixed, native test). The fix round found it read the block's ops, not its exit: a returning call then an unresolved jump counted as a failure path. It reads the CFG terminator now (the fact the render pass reads), and the walk closes the fallthrough of a call through a slot the program says never returns, which the image still listed as a successor. Next: `finish` re-derives a block's successors from its terminator where `record` decided them, so a call that runs into another function's entry still lists a successor the walk refused; the image should carry the walk's own. Landed (b3-lint-gaps): both lints read a wildcard nested in `Some(..)` as the default arm and an `Option<&R2ILOp>` scrutinee (the first tree hit, `call_may_not_return` in the lift, now reads `is_call`); `SSAOp::is_control_flow` and `value_use` list every class (`control_operation!`, the trap joins), and `OpTransfer::conditional` with `SSAOp::jump()` replaces the `Branch \| BranchInd` lists in `slot_promotion`, `frame_reach`, `block.rs` and r2dec (`plans_a_call`, `tail_gap`). Next: `obligation.rs`'s exhaustive arm and `proved.rs`/`stack_bound.rs` still name `Branch`, `BranchInd` or `Switch` beside others, and a Dylint denying the jump class waits for them; the render pass's `CallInd` with a `Through` callee does not read its target where `BranchInd` does, which needs a complete indirect call site to show | standing (GT, r2il); `SSAOp` calls done (r2ssa, 2026-10-16) |
| D42 | A refusal is a property of the stage type: a stage built from an exhausted or failed pass cannot be constructed (`Prepared::seal` refuses, so no `TrustedSeal` exists for such a body), and no reader re-checks it; a check at one consumer is a second owner (#273 let three engine readers through a seal only `into_artifact` refused) | done (2026-10-16: `Prepared::seal` and `Sealed::into_artifact` refuse; the flag and its accessors are `pub(crate)` on the stage's facts and on each pass's result; Dylint `exhausted_read_through_sealed_stage`, deny, no site in the tree; it follows receivers, fields, derefs and an immutable `let`, so a chain a function boundary breaks is not seen, and `PreparedFunctionFacts` holds no flag now: its passes and `collect` return the `Result`, and `RecoveredInterface` no longer copies the provisional collection's flag, `function/mod.rs` noting the `Err` (b3-lint-gaps; `DecompilePrepFacts` keeps its own `pub(crate)` flag, which `seal` reads); r2dec's value gap for an unproven tail jump reads r2ssa's refusal, not `UnsupportedInstruction` (B2, 2026-10-16)) |
| D43 | Every gate runs at the tip of a stack and every baseline source is graded: the stack's top PR carries `full-ci` automatically, and `scripts/check_shards.py` fails a baseline source no equivalence shard lists ([verified layers](doc/adr-verified-layers.md); #264's coverage regression and #292's ungraded `atomics.c` showed the class) | GT (`check_shards.py` and its `check-shards` job landed, both baselines' 10 sources are in shards; `stack-tip.yml` and `stack_tip.py` landed with unit tests; the GitHub side is unproven until the workflow's first run on this branch's next push) |
| D44 | The oracle's breadth is ratcheted: `scripts/oracle_breadth.py` counts each vendored specification's mnemonics with an oracle case (x86-64 117 of 1,513, AArch64 489 of 783 recorded) and its constructors (their width and addressing forms), and the uncovered count of either may only fall | VC, GT |
| D45 | A prover has a mutation target and an adversarial table: `cargo mutants` over `tail_count`, `save_area`, the stack-guard scrub, `result_calls` and the cycle fixpoint, a surviving mutant killed or listed with a reason | r2ssa |
| D46 | Moves are separated from noise by paired tests: a nightly job scores a pinned sample against the last passing night, a baseline that advances only on a pass or a bless, and reports against the pinned blessed baseline so slow drift shows (McNemar exact for the perfect rate, a sign test for the value, Holm-adjusted, significant below 0.01), and timing is an A/B in retired instructions with a bootstrap interval ([measurement discipline](doc/adr-measurement-discipline.md), from Kuna's `nightly.md`) | GT |
| D47 | A type or layout change records `improved` and `worse` per project beside the perfect count, layout and nesting F1 for structures, and a pinned project list splits mining from reporting: the mining column gates a PR, the held-out column is nightly and exit only and a regression there opens an investigation; the mined-set comparison two structuring items used (dump every function of bzip2, zlib and grep at O2 and O2-noinline with each build, score with DecBench's GED, pair the two runs for perfect, mean and total GED, gotos, functions with a goto and text changed) now lives in the tree as `tests/decbench/mined_set.py` (`dump`, `score`, `pair`; the pinned `MINING` and `HELD_OUT` lists; `pair` refuses to compare the two; the pairing is unit-tested over synthetic rows and prints the scratch script's numbers on its scores; [README](tests/decbench/README.md)); next: the PR gate and the nightly that call it, `improved` and `worse` per project, the F1s, timing | GT, T |
| D48 | The census audits its own hunks: a per-feature script classifies a moved hunk and clears it alone only where it checks equivalence, every semantic class is read by a human, and a bless needs zero unclassified hunks | GT, first |
| D49 | Every refusal and residual cause has a catalog row (symptom, closing fact, owner) that feeds `?` help; a test fails on a cause with no row | RF |
| D50 | Batch decompilation is a deterministic pool (longest first, the call graph's condensation callee first with a recursive component one task in address order, per-function budget and memory stop, positional merge): one worker and eight give byte-identical output | LX, after the cost path |
| D51 | Published numbers are generated and honest: `Where this stands` is produced from the gates, shows a clean rate (no marked construct) beside `rendered` (bash 38.0%, fedora ls 58.7% against `94% of ls`), and records the DecBench run of 2026-10-23 ([record](tests/decbench/RESULT-2026-10-23.md)), which replaces 2026-10-17 (that run scored no bash row for r2s, kuna or ghidra, because the OOM killer ended the evaluation worker, and counted 692 angr-only bash rows as misses for the other three): on all 6,978 rows r2s is third of four on union-perfect (28.0%; kuna 32.0%, ghidra 29.3%, angr 18.8%), first on `byte_match` (8.4%), third on GED (24.0%; kuna 28.4%) and third on `type_match` (5.2%; kuna 8.5%), and fourth on the 2,736 non-bash rows (25.4% against kuna 30.5%), which closes D10; README, `LICENSE` against `Cargo.toml`, the panic setting text and the x86-32 `decompiles` claim are corrected | GT, first |
| D52 | Types and structure are the first quality gap and carry a number (the same DecBench run, split per function: r2s is the best of four where a function has no goto, 37.4% GED-perfect against angr 34.7%, kuna 34.4%, ghidra 32.1%, but 40.2% of its scored functions contain a goto against 21.1% to 29.6% elsewhere, and a function with a goto is perfect in 4 of 1,054 cases; 48% of its scored functions carry three or more residuals and are perfect in 13.1% against 31.4% with none; the goto share is a symptom, not the metric: removing the terminal-block gotos (40.8% of them) gains one perfect function, and GED scores the missing `&&`/`||`, shared returns, loop rotation and latch copies a goto marks; the targets are return into arms at fallthrough joins (+15 measured, 0 lost), terminal-tail duplication, `&&`/`||` by BDD identity (ceiling 29), sole-predecessor exit inlining, and the three-or-more-residual share from 48% to a stated target, each graded by D47; [analysis](doc/adr-structure-dominator-tree.md)): T, P9, P8 and SD move ahead of LX2, MM and Q reuse in the Order, with a first exit of `type_match` from 328 (5.2%) to at least kuna's 539 of 6,365 (8.5%) on the corrected all-rows DecBench set (non-bash: 91 to kuna's 164 of 2,664), graded by D47's `improved`/`worse`. Measured 2026-10-23: the chains and tails cut gotos by 36% (8,353 to 5,366, non-bash) and moved union-perfect by +6, so structure's remaining gotos are a readability lever and the union levers are D65, D66 and D67's ranked list; the figures above this clause are the corrupted 2026-10-17 population | T, P9, P8, SD |
| D53 | Discovery never returns a silent empty list: a mapped executable range with no seed yields a D29 refusal that `afl`, `afi` and `pddj` print; `r2image` states a Cortex-M vector table as entries; prologues and code pointers are weaker seeds (libflutterapp 0 against radare2's 6,889; RP2040 `main.elf` 1 against 198) | FS, I |
| D54 | A cap that keeps a partial result is an error and a cache outside the query database is an error, enforced: K widens to the optimiser round budget, address provenance, the r2rewrite term budget (r2dec reads its failures), the `COPIES = 8` family and the lift's atomic `DEPTH`; the name interner is scoped to a function; `cache_outside_query_database` covers every crate; D11's lints go to deny with an allowlist that only shrinks; `Held` tables get a byte budget with a recompute-or-refuse policy; K moves ahead of the cost path | K, LX, GT |
| D55 | Wall time: the listing answers from the walked body (`pdf`, `afb`, `afi` 4 to 5 times slower than radare2 on the bench protocol), and D50's pool design (threads over a `Send` database with shared `Held` summaries, or processes) is decided before D50 starts (gzdoom decompile-all 758.7 s serial against Kuna's 155 s wall at ten workers); the bench records RSS and a decompile-all column; `ax` and gzdoom enter `TIMED` | Q, LX |
| D56 | The product surface has dates: user facts (rename, retype, comment, save) as query-database inputs with a `Basis` (ADR first), `j` on every verb, an embedded agent skill with a test that every verb it names exists, a thin MCP server, an agent-task bench of about 30 questions before A, an i386 equivalence target, cited Windows API rows, library identification by structural hashes, demangling as a `Symbol` hint | V, S1, A, I, P7 |
| D57 | The loop reaches the field and the repository: a merge cadence (a cap of 8 open stacked PRs, a merge queue, bottom-first on green), a red nightly opens an issue within 24 hours, the bench lives in the tree and its nightly builds the branch tip, artifacts leave contabo, a release binary per tag with an opt-in friction record, a fuzz and sanitizer gate for untrusted input, and a quarterly field note on IDA, Binary Ninja, Ghidra, angr, Kuna, rizin and radare2 | GT, maintainer |
| D58 | A rendering decision depends on facts, not on the order placement writes blocks. Done (2026-10-23, [adr-decompiler-rewrite](doc/adr-decompiler-rewrite.md) "D2's spelling order"): `render::spelled::spell` spells every placed block once, in the CFG's reverse postorder, before `control::Writer` places any (`O(B + E + statements + phi inputs)`, no fixpoint), and the Writer holds no `Values` (`crates/r2dec/tests/architecture.rs`); a merge reads as a variable only when every forward edge into its block renders its copy (`Values::owed`; a back edge, a self copy and a gap pay nothing; an edge that retreats in reverse postorder in an irreducible region is read on its promise and the function is spelled again if that copy gaps, `spell_function`: 43 of 2,508 functions hold one, 1 is spelled again). Gate: `census.py orders` over a `--features placement_mutants` build (O1 keeps every loop exit after its loop, O2 reverses each loop's exits, O3 each region's merges) on bash, ls-toybox and pigz: 0 differ under all three, against 0, 120 and 187 with the knobs alone; the text moves in 64, 332 and 720 functions, so the knobs are live; 1 (O2) and 7 (O3) differ only in a selection the structurer chose (`x = c ? a : b`), which reads the layout; five engine tests under the four orders, each a fresh program (the 43, 48 and 24 byte merges that fail before the pass or the rule, a merge whose copies all render, and two irreducible cycles). Natural order against the base, bash, ls-toybox and pigz: 13 of 2,508 functions move, +9 residual sites (seven bash functions' block-transfer locals are numbered in reverse postorder, bash `0x8ee40` lists its `_next` temporaries in another order, five hold a gapped forward copy: bash `0x2dd90`, `0x48140`, `0x85d70`, ls-toybox `0x392dc`, `0x501c8`); 0pack (8,437 functions) 47,850 to 47,898 residual sites (27 functions move: 26 more sites, none fewer) and pumasim (14,776) 67,628 to 67,644 (15 move: 8 more, 2 fewer: `0x279d80` and `0x27b040`, whose `atan2f` read a lane its block does not dominate as a variable, wrong C that D68 replaces with the carrier that dominates it), the biggest movers (0pack `0x4e46d0` 17 to 20, pumasim `0x3f9610` 5 to 10, `0x23b6c0` 3 to 7) each a merge with a gapped forward copy read as residual; none goes rendered to refused, the 442 census goldens and `stable_temporaries` hold byte for byte, certification is 50 rendered, 30 refused, 0 invented, dropped or panicked, as at the base. 0pack `0x476982` has 9 residual sites under all four orders (the base's 7 under O1 read `rsp_8` after a loop whose entry copy at `0x4769b1` is a gap, which nothing assigns on a zero-trip path); bash `0x3c2b0` 0, `0x26760` 221 (1,026 residual obligations) and `0x61520` 4 sites (13 obligations) under every order, `0x85d70` 44 sites (42 at the base: two `rsp_8` reads, entry copy a gap at `0x85de0`). Release `pdd` instructions retired against the base within -0.12% to +0.13% on 0pack `0x62ecf0`, `0x58e3d0`, `0x4e5f90` and pumasim `0x23fdf0`, `0x1fafb0`, `0x2312c0`, peak RSS within the run to run spread (264 to 317 MB both). Left: D62 (a callee's result presence), D63 (any rendered forward copy), D64 (`Values` immutable once spelled); the done text before this is in [doc/roadmap-history.md](doc/roadmap-history.md) | done (SD); left D62, D63, D64; two numbers and the 2 fewer corrected by D68 |
| D59 | A condition chain whose exit is a `continue` to its own head is certified | done (2026-10-23, branch `d59-d60-guards`): `condition::exits` takes an edge to a later test as an edge of the chain's DAG and any other edge to a member (the head, an earlier test) as an exit, as a lone test always did, so the `ExitCount { found: 1 }` fault no longer reverts every chain of the function; the engine test `a_chain_whose_exit_is_a_continue_to_its_own_head_is_one_test` (a scan to a zero byte or a colon: two tests in a `for (;;)` at the base, `do { ... } while (c != 0 && c != 58)` now) and the certificate test `a_chain_whose_second_test_goes_back_to_its_head_has_the_head_as_an_exit` fail without it. Repro of the row: `R2DEC_TRACE_REFUSAL=1 r2s -q -c "aa; pdd @ 0x2e540" bash` no longer prints the revert; `fedora_32_bin_ls` `0xe690` and `0x11ef0` likewise. Over bash, ld-2.31.so, fedora_32_bin_ls, ls-toybox, pigz, bashbot O0 and static-glibc (3,765 functions) 12 change, none newly refused, no goto or residual site added; 11 move only control lines (more `&&`, `do`/`while` for `for (;;)`, fewer `break`, the statements equal) and pigz `get_header` writes one copy fewer of a two-statement tail; bash `extract_colon_unit` runs as at the base on 48 vectors; golden and stable_temporaries are unmoved. D59's program does not reach `rotate::negated`'s De Morgan branch (its continue carries a copy, so the chain is spelled on the staying side): D60 c. Over every function of 0pack and pumasim (23,213) 8 change, all the same loops, none newly refused. The DecBench mined set is not re-scored here | SD |
| D60 | The structurer's data guards each have an end to end engine test and the certificate reads what a copy says | done (2026-10-23, branch `d59-d60-guards`): (a) one engine test per latch-hoist guard, each failing with only its guard removed: L4 `a_copy_stays_on_its_arm_when_the_test_reads_the_variable_it_writes`, L2 `a_copy_stays_on_its_arm_when_code_after_the_loop_reads_what_it_writes`, L3 `a_copy_stays_on_its_arm_when_the_test_is_not_a_proven_read`; (b) `I_T` in `structure/certify.rs`: every statement the placement wrote for a block is written, and as often as any other of it (edge copies are marked statements of the block they leave, with no instruction and no place in the walk; `O(statements)`), 0 of 3,765 functions newly refused or changed, no Statement violation at the base, 23,213 functions of 0pack and pumasim change only as D59 does; with the previous skeptic's drop mutants run again through the corpus: L5 (the hoist drops its copies) 543 functions change and the certificate reverts the stage in all 543 (388 of 388 passed before), T2 (a tail copy drops its first statement) 488 change and 488 are refused (73 of 280 passed), T12 (a joined return's copy drops its first statement) 204 change and 204 are refused (144 of 144 before); (c) the De Morgan branch is reached by a unit test (`rotate::tests::a_loop_that_leaves_on_a_chain_stays_on_its_negation_with_the_connective_flipped`) and an engine test (`a_loop_that_leaves_on_a_chain_stays_on_both_tests_negated`: a chain with nothing copied on the way round), each failing with R1; (d) `owes_only_its_branch`, `NotInert` and the certificate's `inert` parameter are deleted: forced to `true` the fact moved 0 of 3,765 renderings and a condition holds a load in none, the text decides who may be a member, and a member's lost statement is now a Statement violation; (e) C1 `exact` and T3 `named` were dead and are gone, C4 asserts the chain is the one violation, T9 is equal (it changes which arm end keeps the original's markers), V2 went with (d). The sweep again, 61 mutants: 5 no longer apply, 42 of the other 56 die to r2dec unit tests, 8 to engine tests, C3, T7 and T13 to the golden census only, and S5, S6 and T9 survived all three; S5 and S6 (`statement_role` without the never-returns evidence, at the final certificate and at a stage) now die to the engine test `an_arm_ending_in_a_call_declared_never_to_return_is_a_conjunction_that_leaves_by_it` (S5 refuses the function, S6 reverts the chain), and T9 is equal. Release `pdd` on 0pack `0x62ecf0` and pumasim `0x23fdf0`: -0.1% to +0.1% instructions retired, RSS flat, the renderings byte-identical; exit bar: workspace suite 2,977 passed, 0 failed, 8 ignored, certification 50 rendered, 30 refused, 0 invented, 0 dropped, Dylint 12, structure counts unchanged. Left: D61 | SD |
| D61 | A copy's place and text are read, not only that its statements are written, and a copy may carry a residual. (1) `I_T` counts a block's statements across its occurrences: a statement moved from one copy of a block to another, or written twice in one occurrence, leaves the counts equal, and a block of one statement has no sibling to differ from; attribute each statement to the occurrence the walk has open (`Walker::walk_observed`) and refuse a root twice in one occurrence. D58's `Spelled` table holds each block's statements once, which is what that check reads. (2) A copy's text is not compared with its original's (`remint` clones the expressions, so only a producer that rewrites a copy differs): compare the unobserved leaves of one statement across its occurrences (`CExpr` holds `f64`, so a NaN literal needs an order that equals itself). (3) The cost D60 kept: a skipped block that holds a residual is no longer copied into two arms, so 6 of 3,288 functions gain a goto (bash `0x65bf0` 0 to 1, `0x67b10`, `0x67ca0`; ls-toybox `0x5d458`; pigz `0x4115f0`, `0x411a40`; 873 lose gotos, 15,429 to 11,914 in all), until a copy can carry a residual without counting its obligation twice; the stack adds one, bash `0x48140` (1 to 2 gotos at the tip, `R2DEC_TRACE_REFUSAL=1 r2s -q -c "aa; pdd @ 0x48140" bash`): D58's rule reads its tail's `r13_4` as a residual, and a tail that holds one is not copied. (4) Guards that only the census holds: C3 (`leaves_to` of a chain in the walk, 1 golden), T7 (the join needs two arm ends, 5 goldens) and T13 (`replace_gotos` under a switch's arms, 3 goldens) die to no unit or engine test; an engine program each. Gate: mutants that move a statement between copies and that rewrite a copy's operand, each refused by the certificate; one engine test per guard of (4) that fails with it removed (C3, T7 and T13); (3) a bash `0x65bf0` golden, and one for `0x48140`, that read without the goto. Owner: `r2dec` `structure/certify.rs`; (3) `render::values` for the residual's one cell; timing: (1) and (2) conditional on a producer that does either (none does), (3) and (4) next | SD |
| D62 | A callee's result presence is one fact per callee, stated by r2ssa and joined by r2engine's `CallBoundary` (D166) and projected by r2types, not each call site's to spell in r2dec. r2ssa states per call site how the result register's value after the call is read: Dead, ReturnedOnly (with whether the caller's own return type is proven) or Consumed by an operation, transitively through copies and phis, once per function in `O(values + uses)`; r2types joins the site facts per callee in `CalleeResolutionFacts` (a stated prototype or the callee body's carriers outrank site evidence, then Consumed, then ReturnedOnly, then Dead); `Values::decide_prototypes` reads that join and breaks ties by address only. Not a downstream vote: a Readers vote in r2dec rebuilds an r2ssa fact, and a vote that lets the first call with a result win makes pumasim `png_free` `uint64_t`, which doc/adr-decompiler-rewrite.md D4 calls no new evidence. Repro: bash `pdd @ 0x00061520` (`ignore_signal`) declares `void set_signal_handler(uint64_t, uint64_t)` from the first call in address order, `RAX_3` is never assigned behind the call that reads it, 4 residual sites and 13 residual obligations (12 at master); the 2026-10-23 address-order declaration moved 22 of 13,093 declarations (14 `void` to a value, 8 a value to `void`), and pumasim `0x40c140` (`QPointArray::setPoint`, 14 disagreeing sites against 46), `0x34c050` (`QGDict::look_ptr`, 6 more never-assigned reads), 0pack `0x66d2b0`, `0x66f040` and `0x670e80` (`MessageBuilder::operator<<`, 5 against 15) are the same disagreement. First step: read why `callee_carrier_signature` or `Prototype::of` gives no `CallPlan.declared` at bash `0x61520` (the `staged-call-prototype` refusal evidence); where a prototype is stated every site agrees and the tie break never fires. Gate: bash `0x61520` loses its residual obligations without a new `void` to value flip elsewhere in the census, and an engine test that pumasim `png_free` (`0x003b6b20`) stays `void` with 23 residual sites; owners `r2ssa`, `r2types`, `r2dec`; timing: next S7 (2026-11-10): one result per callee at every call; no site reads choose a register. `mint_recovered_call_site_interface` takes no reads, a pair is the callee's at every call, and r2engine's closed-world `CallBoundary` decides `Pair` where a call reads the second register. bash `0x61520` prints `set_signal_handler(uint32_t, uint64_t)` at every call, cold and under `aa`, with 2 residual obligations as base. pumasim `png_free` stays `void` with 23 residual sites. | SD; built (S7) |
| D63 | A merge with one gapped forward copy and one rendered one reads residual (D58 requires every forward edge to render its copy), though a gap traps where it stands. Relaxing to any rendered copy is sound at run time only while the emitted trap and the certificate agree: `GapKind::ValuesNotRendered.ends_control()` is false and `render::traps` excludes it, so the certificate lets control go on past a gap. Relax only if the certificate model changes to say a gap ends control, and a count of merges with at least one rendered and one gapped forward copy (`R2DEC_TRACE_REFUSAL` prints `gapped-forward-copy` per edge: 426 over bash, ls-toybox and pigz at D58's landing) shows many are lost; gate: bash `0x2dd90` (`rbx_2`) and `0x48140` (`r13_4`) read residual under the rule and plain at the base, so read which of their merges mix a rendered and a gapped copy first; owners `r2ssa` (the certificate), `r2dec`; timing: conditional | SD, conditional |
| D64 | `Values` keeps interior mutability (`RefCell` scratch for rendered, residual, effect, restored, owed, broken and the pending attempt, and the block being spelled, a `Cell`) and the D58 spelling pass is its only mutator; making the pass `&mut self` and `Values` immutable once spelled, with each attempt of `spell_function` a value rather than fresh mutable state, makes the single owner true by the type and not by the architecture test. Gate: no `RefCell` in `render::values` outside the pass; owner `r2dec` `render`; timing: deferred | SD, deferred |
| D65 | A function is an import's stub only where the program says its address is one and its body is only the transfer; the route asked the body alone, so a `.text` function that ends in a jump to an import's stub printed "import stub `free` has no body" and no C, though `afl` lists it as no import. Done (2026-10-23, branch `d65-stub-one-jump`, [adr-decompiler-rewrite](doc/adr-decompiler-rewrite.md) "The import-stub route"): `native::sealed` asks `Program::import_at(entry)` (discovery's `Linkage`, `O(log stubs)`) and `SealedFunctionAnalysis::stub_of` runs `stub::import_stub` only for a stated import, naming the stub as discovery does; `seal_function_from_input` and `decompile_function_from_input`, which have no program, no longer take the route. bzip2 `default_bzfree` (`0x5b00`: `test rsi, rsi; je; mov rdi, rsi; jmp free`) and `default_bzalloc` (`0x5b20`: `imul esi, edx; movsxd rdi, esi; jmp malloc`) now render the test and the `free` call, and `malloc` of the product, with the unread-parameter arguments as residuals. The 63 DecBench rows (2026-10-23): 52 write an argument register before the jump, 2 test first, 9 are a bare `jmp` to the stub; all 63 render, 22 without a residual, 24 with a parameter the body reads and nothing declares (P7), 16 with an `int` result whose upper half is a residual (W47), 1 with both. Over bash, ls-toybox, pigz, 0pack and pumasim (26,935 functions) 1,415 rendered as an import stub at the base: 1,200 are `afl`'s `sym.imp.*` and stay stubs, 215 are functions with a name or a body of their own (0pack 100, pumasim 90, bash 13, ls-toybox 12) and render; none is newly refused, no goto is added, +86 residual sites, and the base declared them away wrongly (pumasim `QLayoutItem::~QLayoutItem()` deleting destructor `0x295d10` printed as `extern void _ZdlPvm(void*, uint64_t)`, its constant size dropped; a static initialiser's `__cxa_atexit(...)` as the import itself). The census (442 goldens, 24 stable-temporaries) is unmoved, and the engine tests `a_guarded_wrapper_of_a_stub_is_a_body_not_the_stub`, `a_wrapper_that_computes_the_stub_s_argument_is_a_body_not_the_stub` and `a_function_that_only_jumps_to_a_stub_is_a_body_not_the_stub` fail at the base while `an_import_stub_is_its_import_s_declaration` and `an_effect_before_the_jump_keeps_a_stub_a_body` hold. Measured on DecBench 2026-10-24 ([record](tests/decbench/RESULT-2026-10-24.md)): the 86 import-stub refusals of the 2026-10-23 census are rendered and the census holds none, r2s union-perfect goes 1,899 to 1,965 (+66, 28.0% to 28.9%, still third, 26 rows behind ghidra and 204 behind kuna), GED perfect third to second, and no-output goes 380 to 208 (each rendered function removes a hex-named row and a DWARF-named row, so 380 less 2 x 86; the estimate of about 317 counted the 63 wrapper rows once); the other 50 text changes of D58 and D68 over 6,598 shared functions move no perfect count. Left: `stub::import_stub` keeps its body proof as the second half (`stub_start` already stops a container stub at any write the transfer does not read, so the two agree on every stub discovery names); a linkage section `section_stubs` rejects now renders bodies with a visible gap, a discovery defect `afl` and `pdd` both show | FS |
| D66 | A run that loses rows fails, and the backend checkpoints by time. `tests/decbench/run_gate.py` stops a run (exit 71) when a binary a decompiler rendered has no score key of that decompiler in `evaluated/<binary>.toml` (the 16-byte `binary = "bash"` of 2026-10-17: 55 of 192 cells of that tree, all bash) and refuses more than one evaluation worker (exit 72) for a binary whose decompiled C exceeds 8 MB (one worker holds 5.2 GB for r2s's bash), in `run_decbench.sh`, in the driver before `evaluate_project` (as `SystemExit`, which the driver's `except Exception` does not swallow) and in `evaluate_checkpointed.py`; `report_decbench.py` compares decompilers over the rows every one scored, prints the rows it left out and each decompiler's rows without a score, and exits 73 when one scored nothing in a project (it stops at `kuna scored 0 of 4144 rows of bash` on the 2026-10-17 results and passes the 2026-10-23 ones); `r2sleigh_raw.py` pickles its partial result every 30 s and at the end, not per function (2.5 MB each on bzip2, 18 MB on bash, quadratic: the bash backend took 1,253 s and 1,723 s against about 140 s for r2s alone) and resumes from a checkpoint of the same build, binary and targets. `r2s_batch.py` gave `on_answer` a process's answers only after the process had ended, so the per-function checkpoint never held an answer of a killed run; it streams them now. Next: a host run of bash with the 30 s backend, to put the wall time against r2s's 140 s | GT, immediate (landed on decbench-2, unit-tested; the driver wiring is proven by `--plan` and a stand-in, not a driver run) |
| D67 | The DecBench-ranked fixes after D65 and D66, each with the ceiling the 2026-10-23 run measured (the typed-values design of the same day replaced the type-class ceilings by measured expectations: D71 about +20 net union rows, D72 +7 to +12, D73 5 to 20, together about +27 to +32 against the 270 rows between r2s and kuna, so these items buy `type_match` and clean declarations and do not close the union gap; the residual share is the larger measured lever; D75's built noreturn guard moved the mined set by -123 total GED and 7 perfect rows, and its select ADR recommends no change, see D70 to D75) ([record](tests/decbench/RESULT-2026-10-23.md), section 5), graded by D47 and the source-gold soundness gate. (1) Library prototypes into values: r2abi states pointer-bearing prototypes for the 23 stdio and multibyte imports (945 renderings) and `error`'s `...`, r2types types a value by the callee parameter it flows into and a call result by its return type; 248 pointer-as-integer and 36 library-named functions where it is the only cause, 123 net union rows. (2) A proven return or `void` (W47): about +35 GED-perfect non-bash rows, a size-controlled estimate and not a counterfactual. (3) Widths from reads (W49, W50): 209 functions, 142 net union rows. (4) One C object per SSA web, stated by r2ssa and lowered by r2dec: 138 functions, 126 net union rows, and bash's C from 13.9 MB toward 2.6 MB; unproven for GED, graded on the mined set first. (5) Parameters that flow into residual calls (P7 call contracts): 402 functions, 138 only cause; the 410 functions whose parameter nothing reads need caller or function-pointer-slot evidence from r2engine and have no ceiling yet. (6) Structurer respellings: the noreturn arm as a guard, built (D75: counterfactual +12 / -1, built -123 total GED and +7 perfect rows with 0 lost on the mined set), the select respelling (counterfactual +15 / 0) kept as `?:` on D75's ADR (2,100 of the 2,708 statements with a `?:` hold the renderer's own overshift guards and 50 are branch-born, and respelling those 50 moves GED +59), the do-while rotation's 10 lost rows when the bound is a constant. (7) Unexplained: r2s's mean GED is 39.0 on the non-bash rows against 18.7 to 20.9 for the others while its perfect count is competitive; find what drives the tail (the 380 no-output rows, the large functions) before choosing a fix. Deferred, as benchmark facts or without a shape to copy: large functions (1,345 rows of 20 blocks or more, 2 perfect for any tool), switches (115 rows), compound conditions (75), the project-named type cap (3,020 functions) and the unobservable cap (2,255) | T, P7, SD |
| D68 | A named value is read as a variable only where its definition dominates the read. Done (2026-10-23, branch `d68-dominating-reads`, [adr-decompiler-rewrite](doc/adr-decompiler-rewrite.md) "A name is read where its definition has run"). The skeptic's finding at D58's tip `15486ea7`: `narrowed_argument` took the first value of a lane's class in definition order as a call argument, a value that can be defined where the call is not dominated (pumasim `0x279d80` and `0x27b040`, 4 reads of `tmp_lane_..` assigned only on the other arm of a branch), and D58's reverse postorder spelling read it as a variable; the parents print the same wrong C on a mirrored layout and a residual on another, so D58 moved which functions show a fact error of r2ssa's. Fixed at the owners. r2ssa: `integrity::defined_before` (an entry value, a phi or an earlier operation of the block, a strictly dominating block; `O(1)`) keeps a lane only where its definition has run at the call and otherwise states the carrier the walk reached (`(uint32_t)xmm1_6`); `validate_boundary_values` checks every value a sealed fact reads (call arguments and carriers, call targets, return values, return address, exit stack pointer, branch conditions, switch selectors; `O(facts)`) and refuses the function otherwise (`IntegrityRule::BoundaryValueNotDominated`; call results are definitions and a comparison's operands are copy-class names, so neither is a read). r2dec: `read_name` keeps its own rule as a hard guard, not a `debug_assert` (a residual with refusal evidence and a proof-line cause), asking r2ssa's `value_defined_before` at the block being spelled. Gate: three engine tests under the four write orders (`a_call_argument_is_never_a_lane_that_only_the_other_arm_assigns_under_every_write_order` fails at `15486ea7` on the skeptic's `lane_nondom9` and `lane_nondom7`; with the pick and the check both off, `fact_mutants`, the guard alone makes the read a residual; with the pick off and the check kept, r2ssa refuses), five r2ssa tests (`function::tests::lanes`), the guard's table in r2dec; `scripts/mutants_d68.py`, 25 mutants, 22 killed and 3 alive with reasons. Measured over 25,735 functions of bash, ls-toybox, pigz, 0pack and pumasim and 14,034 of ten more: 2 change (pumasim `0x279d80`, `0x27b040`), none is newly refused, residual sites and gotos equal; the dominance audit reads 0 names out of scope under all four write orders (4 in 2 functions at the tip) and clang `-Wuninitialized` flags 56 of 1,920 generated float functions at the tip and 0 now, under all four write orders; `census.py orders` over 2,508 functions holds at 0, 0, 0 differing, now over refused functions' ledgers too. Also: the production write order (exits inlined, no list permuted) is compiled under test and pinned, and `census.py orders` fingerprints a refused function, which moved 7 guards to tip unseen. Left: a later member of the lane's class that dominates the call is not looked for (`low_lanes` holds the first of each class); the `canonical_source` of a stack reload and a comparison's operands are first-of-class names no renderer reads, unchecked; certificates repeat their facts' values and are not checked on their own | done (SD) |
| D69 | A function a change moves is executed against its base on real binaries, as a standing exit gate. Today the equivalence ratchet's 828 records come from eight small C files (`tests/corpus`, `tests/gold`), so none of the real-binary functions the structurer items change is covered by it: D58 moved 55, D59 12, D65 215 (2026-10-23). The skeptics ran the check by hand in scratch (`difftest2` over the changed lists: native vectors with a trap-aware harness, a control mutant that must show DIFF on 100 of 100 vectors), and found D58's wrong C by a second oracle that the 442 goldens, the 2,998 tests, the census and the equivalence run all missed (clang `-Wuninitialized` with the residual helpers marked noreturn, plus a dominance audit of every variable read). Move both into the tree: the changed-function differential execution beside `tests/equiv`, the uninitialized-read sweep into certification (`certify_render.py`), each with a control that must fail | GT |
| D70 | The soundness gate can grade what the typed-values items change. Done (2026-10-24, branch `d70-gate`; scripts, Python tests and gold data, no Rust; reviewed eight times: the third review's five blockers are closed by one default-deny predicate, the fourth review's three by one rule, a fact DWARF states in another place is no fact here (7), the fifth to eighth reviews' floor by one rule, every function with code counts, and code is what a loaded segment maps from file bytes (9 to 11); the cases graded fall, 4,663 to 3,613 DWARF functions at -O2, and the code still grows since the second round, `differential_truth.py` 428 to 498 lines, `dwarf.py` 632 to 719 and `c_types.py` 410 to 426). Owners: `scripts/differential_truth.py` (scoring, and `declared`, the one predicate), `scripts/c_types.py` (the typed prototype, one reader for r2s's header and for DWARF), `tests/equiv/dwarf.py` (the oracle's DWARF reader, which places a function) and `tests/equiv/r2s_batch.py` (the `pddj` reader the equivalence gate and DecBench share). Invariant: a fact is graded only if DWARF states it and the container agrees; every other function is named and graded as nothing (AGENTS rule 8). Cost: one pass over the DIEs, the functions and the symbols of each binary, and one `pddj` process per graded function. (1) The truth is what the debug build's DWARF declares, and a function is graded only if it passes `differential_truth.declared`: gcc produced the DWARF (`DW_AT_producer` is `GNU C` and a digit), the DIE states itself (no `DW_AT_abstract_origin` or `DW_AT_specification`), it has `DW_AT_prototyped`, and its result and every parameter are void, an integer, a bool, an enum or a pointer (typedefs resolved, an array or function decayed, `const` and `volatile` kept, a struct its tag or its typedef). Every other function is named in the report's `ungradable` under the first check it fails, graded as nothing and no failure of the binary; a debug build with no gradable function exits 2, as the base did, and so does a `gcc -g1` build, which keeps no prototype, type or parameter and was graded as `void f(void)`. The gate owns no ABI class for an aggregate, a vector, a float, a long double, an `__int128` or a complex number, on x86-64 or AArch64 (the table is empty and the code says so), so a function with one is ungradable whole: a struct result over 16 bytes takes the first integer register on x86-64 and x8 on AArch64, and DWARF's order scored it as a shift (`make_big(int n, long *p)` prints as `uint64_t fcn_1490(void* arg0, uint32_t arg1, uint64_t* arg2)` and the gate compared `n` with `void*`; `vec_mid(int a, v4si v, int b)` prints as `(uint32_t, uint32_t)` and the vector was read as `int32_t*`). Three choices are stricter than the review asked, each for a mechanism: a K&R definition with no prototype in scope is ungradable although it has formals (its caller promotes `char c` to int, DWARF says char, and gcc writes no `DW_AT_prototyped`); C++ is ungradable (it states no `DW_AT_prototyped`, and `GNU C++` fails the producer test); clang is ungradable (its dead-argument elimination removes `unused` from `helper(int a, int unused, int c)` in place, so the function takes rdi and rsi, keeps its name and lists three formals with no marker, checked on `dae.c`; `-flto` units are `GNU GIMPLE` and fail the same test). An instance of an origin is ungradable because a gcc clone lists the source's formals in its own order and keeps a removed constant: `scale.constprop.0.isra.0` takes (n, p) and its DIE lists n, k, p. A type the gate cannot state refuses the function, and a reference into a type unit (`DW_FORM_ref_sig8`) is a type in another unit, where it read as `void`; an aggregate with neither a tag nor a typedef name is no longer spelled `struct <anonymous>`. Measured on the 24 DecBench -O2 binaries, 4,663 DWARF functions: 826 instances of an origin (17.7%: 170 sit at a dotted symbol such as `.constprop.0`, 652 at a plain one, as an out-of-line copy of a function inlined elsewhere, and 4 at neither), 198 without `DW_AT_prototyped` (K&R helpers of the bash tree; `mksyntax`, `mksignames` and `psize.aux` are made only of them), 26 with a type of no stated class (12 with an aggregate, 1 with a float, 13 with a pointer to a struct declared and not defined), 3,613 graded (77.5%); at -O2-noinline, 6,096 functions: 282, 292, 58 and 5,464 graded (89.6%). (2) A function is placed at its DWARF entry (`DW_AT_entry_pc`, else `DW_AT_low_pc`, else the first range of `DW_AT_ranges`, in `.debug_ranges` and `.debug_rnglists`, including the `rnglistx` form; a constant `entry_pc` is an offset and unplaces), and only where a FUNC symbol of the debug build's `.symtab` starts (`function_symbols`, which lists no import: an undefined symbol has value 0 and would have placed every discarded function). The first range is not the entry in clang `-fbasic-block-sections=all` (cold.c: `checked_div` lists 0x11cd first, a NOTYPE `checked_div.__part.1`, and its FUNC symbol 0x11c0 last), so the round-two rule graded fragments (`pick return: expected uint32_t, said void` on abi.c, `tail parameter 0` and `is_set return`); on cold.c none of the four functions is placed now. 39 functions in 12 of the 24 DecBench -O2 binaries have ranges and no `low_pc`, a cold path split off by `abort()` or `exit()`, and all are placed at the hot part, which gcc lists first and which starts a FUNC symbol: none of the 4,663 is unplaced. A function the gate cannot place, two functions at one entry and a function the engine's debug listing lacks are `ungradable` too, with their own reasons. Exit 2 stays for an answer or a DWARF file the gate cannot read, a stripped build that carries DWARF (it passed at soundness 1.0 against its own input) and an engine that recovers no function. `Dwarf.subprograms()` is unchanged for the equivalence gate (D85). A stripped build whose `.gnu_debuglink` names a file readelf finds is read with that file's DWARF and refused as carrying DWARF. (3) The header is a parsed C declarator (`c_types.parse_prototype`: pointers with qualifiers, groups, arrays, function suffixes, `...`; r2s writes a function pointer result as a type name and then the name, `int32_t(*)(int32_t, int32_t) pick(int32_t sel)`). `unsigned long`, `size_t` and `uint64_t` are one type, a qualifier on a whole parameter or result is no part of it, a `...` is a flag, and the signature must name the record's `definition` (890 of 890 renderings in six binaries). (4) The marker is `residuals`, the list of every residual in the code, and no longer `proof.residual`, which counts the obligations a residual stands for and is 0 for a control gap: 5 of 800 rendered functions in six -O2 binaries (cmp 0x3b10 and 0x3cf0, grep 0x6ac0, 0x7d60 and 0x7e50) carry a residual the count does not (`TailTransferNotRendered`, `r2sleigh_residual_void(1)` in the code). (5) Every wrong fact is bucketed per role in `silent_buckets` and `marked_buckets` (`void_vs_value`, `pointer_as_integer`, `integer_as_pointer`, `qualifier`, `pointee`, `width_wider`, `width_narrower`, `sign`, `other`); `return_presence` counts void to void, void to value, value to void and value to value with the silent share; `soundness_without_qualifier` is the rate without the silent `qualifier` rows (D92); a baseline compares its silent errors as a multiset, and a key needs a cause of at least three words that does not open with TODO, TBD, FIXME, XXX or WIP. (6) Any exception that is no verdict is exit 2 with the traceback on stderr (CI accepts exit 0 and 1, and a traceback exits 1): an `aflj` name that is no string, a baseline that is no JSON and a header nested 5,000 deep were all exit 1 or a traceback. The gate runs `pddj` once per graded function of the stripped build and `aflj` twice; an `r2s: ` line on stderr is a decline and any other stderr line is ignored. `tests/gold/source_gold/returns.c` has its own baseline and runs after the hashes step; `cold.c`, `odd.c`, `seven.c`, `gcsect.c`, `clones.c` and `abi.c` run in the same job and must exit 0 or 1, `cold.c` built with `-g1` must exit 2 with the refusal on stderr, and so must odd.c and abi.c built with `-fdebug-types-section`, naming the type unit. Mutation: `scripts/mutants_d70.py` applies 574 mutants of the gate, the type model, the entry rule, the symbol rule, the `pddj` contract and `type_causes.py` (the fourth round adds 42 net, one or more per rule of (7)): 570 die, 4 survive and each says why it is equivalent, 0 are wrong (`--check` proves each edit applies once). The fourth round's first full run left 4 alive (no test read an `implicit_const` size, an instance's formals from its origin, a member location that is an expression, or an enum over a pointer in the shared reader); the tests that kill them were added and the 4 die. Measured at the base `a0d9e34a` (Mac release r2s, which is the d70 tree's r2s: no Rust differs) on Ubuntu 24.04 gcc 13.3.0 default-PIE builds, which are what CI and both baselines use (`gcc -g -O0` or `-O2 -o dbg src.c; strip -o str dbg`). Soundness references for D71 and D73 (silent errors against asserted facts; correct and silent counts), rebuilt on this tree and re-read by the third-round gate: review.c -O0 0.2069 (6, 23), -O2 0.2400 (6, 19); branchy.c -O0 0.0649 (5, 72), -O2 0.0946 (7, 67); hashes.c -O0 0.6875 (33, 15), -O2 0.5652 (26, 20); values.c 0.9500 at both (38, 2); shapes.c -O0 0.8590 (67, 11), -O2 0.8919 (66, 8); returns.c -O0 0.0000 (0, 18), -O2 0.1111 (2, 16). Ten of the twelve did not move; shapes.c did, from 0.8471 (72, 13) and 0.8846 (69, 9), because its struct-by-value functions (`mixed_fold`, `mixed_from` and `wide_make` at -O0, `mixed_fold` and `wide_make` at -O2) are ungradable whole where the round-two gate graded their integer parameters; review.c loses `avg` (a double) and neither of its counts moves. Builds of one flag set can differ by a fact, so read a reference as plus or minus 0.003. Both baselines were re-recorded for the report's new shape (`ungradable` for `without_entry`, `unlisted` and `unreadable_truth`, and `soundness_without_qualifier`) and none of their numbers moved: hashes.c -O0 17 DWARF functions, 33 correct, 0 missing, 3 marked, 15 silent, soundness 0.6875 (0.8462 without the 9 `const` rows); returns.c -O2 12 functions, 2, 0, 6, 16, soundness 0.1111. At the third round the gate exits 0 or 1 on 21 of the 24 DecBench -O2 binaries and 21 of 24 at -O2-noinline ((7) has the fourth round's); the other three of each (bash's `mksyntax`, `mksignames` and `psize.aux`, written in K&R C) have no gradable function and exit 2. -O2 over the 21: 4,654 DWARF functions, 3,626 graded, 3,554 rendered; 975 correct, 1,180 missing, 5,529 marked, 2,829 silent; soundness 0.2563 (the 24 binaries of the second round: 4,663, 4,574, 1,204, 1,494, 6,847, 3,780, 0.2416). -O2-noinline: 6,080, 5,477, 5,407; 1,378, 1,642, 7,843, 4,537; 0.2330 (was 6,096, 6,016, 1,509, 1,958, 8,875, 4,962, 0.2332). The facts the gate no longer grades are 2,812 of 13,325 at -O2 (229 correct, 951 silent), so the soundness rate moved by +0.0147 at -O2 (the 3 exit-2 binaries included in the facts removed) and by -0.0002 at -O2-noinline. The third round's claim that the 269 builds of its review showed no silent error the old gate did not report did not reproduce on type-unit builds (odd.c -O0 -gdwarf-4, abi.c -O0 -gdwarf-5 and an enum source, each with -fdebug-types-section, reported 3, 3 and 5 new keys); those builds are refused now and the claim is restated in (7) on a matrix that was rerun. The CI shapes on the Linux builds (gcc 13.3): cold, odd, seven, gcsect, clones, abi and enums exit 1 (the engine's silent errors), `cold.c -g1` exits 2, and odd.c `-gdwarf-4` and abi.c `-gdwarf-5` with `-fdebug-types-section` exit 2 naming the type unit; `clones.c` names `scale` and `field_b` (2 of 8), `abi.c` 14 + 1 of 26 on x86-64 and the same 15 on AArch64, `odd.c` 6 of 31, `seven.c` 1 of 4, `gcsect.c` `never_called`. By hand: `s 0x1490; pddj` on the stripped `abi.c` prints `uint64_t fcn_1490(void* arg0, uint32_t arg1, uint64_t* arg2)` for `struct big make_big(int n, long *p)`, so r2s sees the hidden result pointer as arg0 and the old gate's DWARF-order truth was wrong; `s 0x1450; pddj` prints `(uint32_t, uint32_t)` for `vec_mid(int, v4si, int)`, whose vector is in a vector register; `readelf --syms` on the clang build lists `checked_div` as FUNC at 0x11c0 and its fragments as NOTYPE. (7) The fourth review (2026-11-09) found three blockers, all from gcc type units, and one rule closes them: DWARF that states a fact in another place states nothing here. A type DIE with `DW_AT_signature` (a type-unit stub) or `DW_AT_declaration` raises `NotExpressible` in `c_types.from_dwarf`; a base type or an enum states its own `DW_AT_byte_size`, and an enum an underlying integer of that size (the defaults `size or 4` and unsigned are deleted, with the test that pinned them). Before, gcc's enum stub, `-gdwarf-2 -gstrict-dwarf` (no `DW_AT_type` under an enum) and `enum e;` read as `uint32_t`: abi.c's `enum wide { W0 = -1, W1 = 5000000000LL }` was graded `uint32_t` against the engine's right `uint64_t`, and enums.c's `enum neg` turned unsigned and agreed with the engine's wrong `uint32_t`. The shared reader takes the same rule (`Dwarf._describe`: an integer or enum with no stated size, an enum with no underlying integer of its size, a stub are `unsupported`); it keeps a struct declared and not defined as `incomplete` (D93). A dump with a type unit (`DW_TAG_type_unit`, in `.debug_types` or a DWARF 5 `DW_UT_type` unit) raises `TYPE_UNIT` and the gate exits 2 naming it: DWARF 4 numbers `.debug_types` from 0, so its DIEs replaced those of `.debug_info` (`main` of odd.c read `(int32_t, int64_t*)`, 4 offsets collided). Two DIEs at one offset are an error too, which also refuses a `-gsplit-dwarf` build whose `.dwo` readelf follows (skeleton and split unit both start at 0x14 in DWARF 5, 0xb in DWARF 4). Found while measuring: the third round's `--debug-dump=no-follow-links` makes binutils 2.42 read every `strx` string without the unit's offsets base, so every clang unit was named `u clang version 18.1.3` and all 828 clang functions of the equivalence population (414 per architecture) left it, unseen because 22 of its tests skip on the Mac. The flag is gone; over the 1,656 functions of that population (tests/corpus and tests/gold, gcc and clang, -O0 to -O2, x86-64 and AArch64) every call spec is now what the base reader describes. The minors are closed by removal: `Die.number` reads only a constant form (`data1` to `data16`, `sdata`, `udata`, `implicit_const`), so an `exprloc` array bound (`int a[n][n]` read as `[4]`, the block's length) refuses the function and a DWARF 2 member offset is its one `DW_OP_plus_uconst` (it read as 2); `DW_AT_ranges` is read in its offset forms; an origin chain that meets a DIE twice raises (`_formals` looped forever) and `_attr_via_origin` lost its cap of 8; the gate refuses a stripped build whose GNU build ID is not the debug build's, or a pair with none (DecBench's 48 pairs share theirs); and a build where fewer than one in three of its functions with code is graded prints no rate and exits 2 ((9) says what has code; the review's s5_misc -O3 graded 3 of 20 and printed soundness 1.0). Measured with one r2s (master `14d384c3b`, release, on the build host) on a rerun matrix of 270 builds: 18 sources (tests/corpus, tests/gold and tests/gold/source_gold) by 15 flag sets (gcc -O0, -O1, -O2, -O3, -Os, -O2 -gdwarf-4, -O0 -gdwarf-4 -fdebug-types-section, -O2 -gdwarf-5 -fdebug-types-section, -O2 -gdwarf-2 -gstrict-dwarf, -O0 -gdwarf-3, -g1, -flto, -gsplit-dwarf, clang -O2, AArch64 gcc -O2). Third-round gate to this one: no traceback; 8 builds keep exit 0, 169 keep 1, 72 keep 2 and 21 go from 1 to 2 (20 with a type unit, and enums.c `-gstrict-dwarf`, 1 of 7 graded, under the floor); in the 177 builds both grade, no silent key is new and 5 leave (2,530 to 2,525), all in the two strict DWARF 2 builds whose enum truth was the default (`next_color`, `pick`, `pickw`). Against the base gate (master's, which took r2s's reading of the debug build as truth), the 177 builds report 354 silent facts the base did not, in 64 builds of 9 sources; read by hand on rec_index, wide_tail, tail, pick, use_field and must_alloc, each is an engine error by the source (`int rec_index(Rec *v, int i, int q)` said `uint32_t (uint32_t*, uint32_t, uint32_t)`, `void must_alloc(size_t n, char **out)` said `uint64_t`), and 157 are `int32_t` said `uint32_t`. So the sentence that holds is: in every build both grade, this gate reports no silent error the third-round gate did not. The twelve soundness references do not move (review.c, branchy.c, hashes.c, values.c, shapes.c and returns.c at -O0 and -O2, same correct and silent counts). By hand: `s 0x1290; pddj` on the stripped enums.c -O2 prints `uint32_t fcn_1290(uint32_t arg0, uint32_t arg1, uint32_t arg2)` for `enum neg e_neg(enum neg a, int b, enum neg c)`, whose body is `test esi, esi; mov eax, edi; cmove eax, edx`: nothing states a sign, so the three `int32_t` facts are silent errors of the engine and the type-unit build had hidden them. DecBench with the same r2s, third-round gate then this one: the third round's numbers reproduce exactly. Now -O2: 19 binaries exit 0 or 1 and 5 exit 2 (`bashversion`, 1 of 4 graded, and the K&R `mkbuiltins`, 7 of 39, join `mksyntax`, `mksignames` and `psize.aux` under the floor); over the 19, 4,611 DWARF functions, 3,605 graded, 3,533 rendered; 972 correct, 1,147 missing, 5,487 marked, 2,828 silent; soundness 0.2558 (was 0.2563). -O2-noinline: 20 and 4 (`mkbuiltins`, 8 of 45); 6,035, 5,456, 5,386; 1,375, 1,608, 7,803, 4,533; 0.2327 (was 0.2330). In the 39 builds both grade no silent key is new (7,361 and 7,361) and no truth of a still-graded function changes; the type rule refuses 13 functions per level, each with a pointer to a struct declared and not defined (`struct __dirstream*` in `opendir_safer`, `opendirat` and `streamsavedir`, `struct XML_ParserStruct*` in six of xmlwf, `struct kwset*` in grep's `kwsinit`, `struct _IO_FILE*` in find's `print_quoted`). (8) The fifth review (2026-11-09) found two blockers. The floor counted every function the gate cannot place, so `gclib.c` (12 unused functions, 4 used, `-ffunction-sections -Wl,--gc-sections`) exited 2 with `4 of 16 functions are gradable`, where the base and the third-round gate exit 1; the fix dropped every unplaced function from the floor, which failed open and which (9) replaces. The second blocker was a claim. A K&R definition with a prototype in scope has `DW_AT_prototyped` and keeps its narrow formals, so it is graded by the types the callee converts to (`krt.c`: `kr_short (a, c) int a; short c;` under `int kr_short (int, int);` is `int32_t (int32_t, int16_t)`, its code `movswl %si,%eax`; r2s says `uint16_t arg1` and its own body reads `(int16_t)arg1`, a sign error of the engine). The review measured 1,387 of the 3,613 graded DecBench -O2 functions (1,590 of 5,464 at -O2-noinline) as such definitions (bash's PARAMS style and zlib), none with a char, short, `_Bool` or float parameter, so no truth changed; DWARF holds no marker that would refuse them, and (1) and the refusal reason say that only a K&R definition with no prototype in scope is refused. Both shapes are fixtures (`gclib.gcc.O2.gc`, `krt.gcc.O2`) and CI builds; the review's 7 live mutants (a constant form `data2`, `data4`, `data8` or `udata`, a DWARF 3 `data4` range list, an op around `DW_OP_plus_uconst`, a build ID compared on a prefix) have tests and die. (9) The sixth review (2026-11-09) reopened the floor: with only placed functions counted, a build linked with `-Wl,-x` (ld `--discard-all`), whose static functions keep their code in `.text` and lose their FUNC symbols, graded 3 of 12 functions (`returns.c`) or 3 of 15 (`statics.c`) and printed a rate. The rule now: the floor counts every DWARF function whose code is in the binary, placed or not. A function had code unless its entry lay in no executable section of the debug build (`readelf --section-headers`, flag X; (11) replaces the test): that DIE, a linker's tombstone (0 under gcc's `--gc-sections`; all ones, unit-tested and not built, as lld writes it), is listed under `DISCARDED` and left out. A DIE with neither `DW_AT_low_pc` nor `DW_AT_ranges` (a declaration, a function only inlined) holds no code and is in no list, as before. Every other unplaced function counts ungraded, whatever its reason, and a section table readelf fails on or that has no executable section is unreadable (exit 2). Measured with one r2s (master `14d384c3b`, Mac release, Apple M5) on gcc 13.3 builds, exit codes base / fifth round / now: `returns.c` and `statics.c` with `-Wl,-x` on x86-64 and AArch64 at -O0 and -O2, and `statics.c` with gc sections and `-Wl,-x` at -O0 and -O2, 1 / 1 / 2, each `3 of 12` or `3 of 15 functions are gradable` with 9 or 12 `NO_FUNC_START`; `gclib.c` with gc sections on x86-64 and AArch64 at -O0 and -O2, and with `-Wl,-x` added on x86-64, 1 / 1 / 1, 4 of 4 graded and 12 `DISCARDED`; `returns.c`, `statics.c` and `gclib.c` without either flag and `krt.c`, at -O0 and -O2, 1 / 1 / 1 with reports unchanged. Builds no review listed agree: `gclib.c` with gc sections through ld.gold (x86-64 -O2, AArch64 -O0) and with `-no-pie` exit 1 with 12 `DISCARDED`; `statics.c` `-Wl,-x` through ld.gold and `returns.c` `-Wl,-x -no-pie` exit 2; clang's builds exit 2 as before, no function passing the producer test. DecBench moves 0: its 48 binaries hold 4,663 (-O2) and 6,096 (-O2-noinline) placed functions and 0 unplaced, and each states its executable sections. Fixtures `returns.gcc.O2.x` and `statics.a64.O0.x`, and a `*.sections.txt` for every dump the gate's tests read ((11) replaces them), rebuilt with a byte-identical symbol table; CI refuses `returns.c` and `statics.c` built with `-Wl,-x` and grades `statics.c`. The 15 mutants of the rule die and the fifth round's `defined = len(placed)` is one of them (595 mutants, 0 wrong, the 4 that live the documented equivalents). (10) The seventh review (2026-11-10) reopened the floor through the section table: a row the parser could not read was skipped, so gcc's `section("\"my text\"")`, a name with a space, made an executable section no code and its 12 statics `DISCARDED`; `statics.c` so built with `-Wl,-x` graded 3 of 15 and exited 0 (x86-64 -O2, -O3) or 1 (-O0, -O1, AArch64 -O0 to -O3). The rule: every numbered row of `readelf --wide --section-headers` reads, from the right where Address, Off, Size, ES, Flg, Lk, Inf and Al have fixed shapes, or the table is unreadable (exit 2). With it the 8 builds exit 2 (`3 of 15 functions are gradable`, 12 `NO_FUNC_START`), and clang's 2 such builds name the same reason and stay refused by producer. Measured with the same r2s and gcc 13.3: the offline floor (`read_dwarf` and `partition`) over the review's 350 builds moves the 8 and only them; the real gate on 17 earlier repros (`-Wl,-x`, gclib gc on x86-64 and AArch64, krt, clones, abi, `-g1`, clang `-fbasic-block-sections`, s4_enum, statics -O2) gives the same exit code and report as before. CI's Equivalence Harness job ran `ExecutableSectionTests`, which also read a lowercase flag (`AXl`; (11) deletes the parser); 6 mutants of the row rule die (601 mutants; the 12 section and 13 floor mutants rerun, 0 wrong). (11) The eighth review (2026-11-10) reopened the floor through the section flags: gcc's injected directive `section(".fdat,\"aw\",@progbits #")` puts 12 statics' code in `.fdat`, flagged WA (A with `"a"`), so they read `DISCARDED` and the build with `-Wl,-x` graded 3 of 15 and exited 0 (x86-64 -O2) or 1 (x86-64 -O0, AArch64 -O0). The rule now: a DWARF function has code when its entry (the one the gate places by, (2)) lies in the file bytes a PT_LOAD maps (`p_vaddr <= pc < p_vaddr + p_filesz`), less the ELF header and program header table, which the first load of a PIE maps at 0 (to 0x318 on x86-64, 13 entries; to 0x238 on AArch64, 9): without that cut the tombstone 0 lies in a load and counts as code. Section flags decide nothing; `executable_sections`, its row parser and the 14 `*.sections.txt` fixtures are deleted. `dwarf.loaded_code` reads the program headers by a struct read of the ELF (32 or 64 bit, either byte order); an ET_REL object (`RELOCATABLE`, named), any type but ET_EXEC or ET_DYN, a cut or `PN_XNUM` table, or no load with file bytes past the headers is unreadable and exits 2 (`UNREADABLE program headers`). Measured on gcc 13.3: every `--gc-sections` tombstone is 0 (bfd PIE and `-no-pie` on x86-64 and AArch64, gold, DWARF 4 and 5) and lies in no loaded code, whose first byte is 0x318, 0x238, 0x400318 or 0x400238. Real gate, one r2s (master `14d384c3b`, Mac release), head `7265de0f2` to now: the review's 5 `.fdat` builds and 6 rebuilt from `tests/gold/source_gold/fdat.c` (x86-64 -O0 and -O2 with `"aw"` and `"a"`, AArch64 -O0 with both) go from exit 0 (4) or 1 (7), 3 of 15 graded, to exit 2, `3 of 15 functions are gradable` with 12 `NO_FUNC_START`; `gcc -c gclib.c` goes from exit 2 on the build ID to exit 2 naming ET_REL; gclib gc (x86-64 -O0 and -O2, AArch64 -O2, gold ICF -O0 and -O2) stays exit 1 with 4 graded and 12 `DISCARDED`, `returns.c` and `statics.c` with `-Wl,-x` (x86-64 -O0 and -O2, AArch64 -O2) and `statics.c` -O2 stay exit 2, and krt, clones and s4_enum stay exit 1, each report identical but the `DISCARDED` wording. DecBench bzip2, gzip and grep at -O2 (one Linux r2s, answers cached across both trees) give byte-identical reports, exit 1, 52, 107 and 261 graded. A test pins that functions sharing an entry count in the floor (the fifth review's live mutant `floor-shared-out` dies); the `fdat` fixtures fail on the head and pass now, and CI refuses `fdat.c` with `-Wl,-x` beside `returns.c` and `statics.c` and runs `LoadedCodeTests`. Mutation: 14 load-rule mutants and the shared-floor mutant are new, 12 section mutants are deleted (604 mutants, 600 die, the 4 documented equivalents live, 0 wrong). Left: D95.
